The Central dialect group (PNT1-PNT4) also yields the highest average harmful-flip rate across all models, at 6.54%. These findings show that strong performance on Standard Vietnamese does not guarantee reliable behavior under meaning-preserving regional variation.

</details>


### [136] [From Reasoning Depth to Reasoning Breadth: Evaluating Multi-Point Associative Reasoning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10444)
*Si'an Xie,Jiaxun Liu,Biao Yang,Wei Yuan,Fan Yang,Tingting Gao,Ming Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MPAR-Bench，一个中英双语基准，通过多点联想推理（multi-point associative reasoning）来单独评估大语言模型的推理广度（reasoning breadth），弥补现有基准只关注推理深度（reasoning depth）的不足。基准包含1,000个由多智能体流程生成、经人类验证的条目，并通过多种扰动测试发现，推理深度的提升并不会自动带来稳健的推理广度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型在推理任务上的进步主要反映了推理深度的提升（即更长的推理链），但推理广度——即并行探索多个语义方向并整合线索的能力——在很大程度上未被研究且未被现有基准覆盖。作者希望填补这一空白，专门评估并测量模型的推理广度能力。

Method: 作者受合作游戏Just One启发，设计MPAR-Bench基准：(1) 每个条目要求模型从多个独立生成的、语义多样的线索中恢复隐藏目标词；(2) 采用多智能体线索生成pipeline构建1,000个条目，配合基于嵌入的多样性过滤和人工验证；(3) 答案空间来自公共词表，但所有线索集均从零生成；(4) 评估维度包括准确率、ANLS、嵌入相似度、推理链验证，以及四种扰动测试（线索遮蔽、顺序打乱、干扰项注入、多步线索）。

Result: 实验结果表明：扰动使英文准确率降低9-18个百分点、中文降低5-12个百分点；思考模式（thinking mode）能提升标准设定下的准确率（尤其在英文上），但并不能一致地降低对扰动的敏感性；案例分析还显示扩展推理可能推翻原本正确的假设。这些结果表明推理深度不会自动转化为稳健的推理广度。

Conclusion: 推理广度是一项与推理深度互补且当前未被充分覆盖的能力，现有模型在推理广度上表现脆弱，容易受到扰动影响。更大的推理深度并不能保证更稳健的推理广度，这为未来LLM推理能力评估和模型改进指出了新的方向。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have made substantial progress on reasoning tasks that require increasingly long and complex inferential chains. This progress primarily reflects reasoning depth. A complementary and comparatively unexamined capability is reasoning breadth: exploring multiple semantic directions in parallel and integrating the resulting clues into one coherent answer. We introduce MPAR-Bench, a bilingual English-Chinese benchmark that isolates reasoning breadth through multi-point associative reasoning. Inspired by the cooperative game Just One, each item asks a model to recover a hidden target from several independently generated, semantically diverse clues. We construct 1,000 items using a multi-agent clue-generation pipeline, embedding-based diversity filtering, and human verification. Only the answer space is drawn from public word lists, whereas every clue set is generated from scratch. Beyond exact-match accuracy, we evaluate models using accuracy, ANLS, embedding similarity, reasoning-trace verification, and four perturbations: clue masking, order shuffling, distractor injection, and multi-step clues. Across evaluated models, perturbations reduce accuracy by 9-18 percentage points in English and 5-12 percentage points in Chinese. Thinking mode improves standard-setting accuracy, especially in English, but does not consistently reduce sensitivity to perturbations. Case-level analysis also shows that extended reasoning can overturn an initially correct hypothesis. These results indicate that greater reasoning depth does not automatically confer robust reasoning breadth, and that reasoning breadth remains largely uncovered by current benchmarks.

</details>


### [137] [MD-ProTector: Positioning Multiple Data-Driven Prototypes for LLM-Generated Text Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10459)
*Jinmo Han,Jimin Hong,Chanyeong Moon,Ju Yeon Kang,Seonuk Kim,Nam Soo Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MD-ProTector，一种基于多原型参考向量的编码器式AI文本检测方法。该方法在编码器嵌入空间为每个类别维护多个可训练的原型向量，以刻画类别内的多样化写作变异，并通过Prototype Positioning损失将类别级结构与类别内变异分离。在五个跨领域、生成器、语言和对抗性变异设置的基准测试中，MD-ProTector在MAGE CDCM和RAID上取得了最高的AvgRec，并在RAID上取得了最高的AUROC和最低的FPR95。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM生成内容日益复杂，检测系统需要在规模上运行并处理多样的写作风格、领域、语言和生成模型。标准的二分类方法只能输出类别标签，无法显式组织类别内部的大量变异，导致检测效果受限。

Method: MD-ProTector在编码器嵌入空间为每个类别引入多个可训练参考向量（即原型），为同一类别内不同文本群体提供独立决策边界。此外，提出Prototype Positioning损失，将类别级结构与会区分不同原型的类别内变异进行解耦，从而确定每个原型应代表的变异模式。

Result: 在三个大型基准测试的五个设置（涵盖领域、生成器、语言和对抗性变异）中，MD-ProTector在MAGE CDCM和RAID上取得最高的AvgRec，并在RAID上取得最高的AUROC和最低的FPR95，优于其他基于编码器的方法。

Conclusion: MD-ProTector通过多原型表示有效建模类别内变异，在多种真实场景下显著提升了AI文本检测性能，证明了捕捉类别内部多样性对于检测系统的重要性。

Abstract: As LLM-generated content becomes more sophisticated, detection systems for distinguishing those texts from human-written text must operate at scale while handling diverse writing styles, domains, languages, and generator models. Input-only encoder detectors are suitable for practical deployment setting, but standard binary classification supplies only the class label and does not explicitly organize the substantial variation within either class. We propose MD-ProTector, which represents each class with multiple trainable reference vectors in the encoder embedding space, referred to as prototypes. These prototypes provide separate decision boundaries for different groups of texts within the same class. However, adding multiple prototypes alone does not determine which variation each prototype should represent. MD-ProTector addresses this problem with Prototype Positioning loss, which separates class-level structure from the within-class variation that differentiates individual prototypes. Evaluated across five settings from three large-scale benchmarks covering domain, generator, language, and adversarial variation, MD-ProTector achieves the highest AvgRec on MAGE CDCM and RAID and the highest AUROC and lowest FPR95 on RAID among the compared encoder-based methods.

</details>


### [138] [Calibrating Post-Training Feature Shifts for LLM Data Contamination Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10462)
*Zhen Yang,Mengqi Wang,Gengda Zhao,Mo Zhou,Jianwei Wang,Wenjie Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出CalibDCD，一个针对基于特征的数据污染检测（DCD）方法的通用校准框架。该框架通过多视角偏移检测识别LLM后训练（如指令微调、偏好优化等）引起的重复性特征偏移，并结合有界特征校正选择性地减轻这些偏移对成员预测的影响，从而提升现有DCD方法在真实场景下的检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代LLM在预训练后会经历后训练阶段（如指令微调、偏好优化、推理导向训练），这些过程会改变模型输出并导致成员特征偏移，降低成员与非成员之间的可分离性，从而削弱现有基于特征的DCD方法的有效性。

Method: CalibDCD由两个核心组件构成：(1) 多视角偏移检测（Multi-View Shift Detection）：在已知非成员文本上评估受控的提示变体，整合信息量最大的视角以识别与后训练相关的重复性特征偏移；(2) 有界特征校正（Bounded Feature Correction）：选择性地调整与检测到的偏移对齐的特征组件，并控制校正程度以保留有用的检测信息。

Result: 实验表明，CalibDCD能够一致性地提升现有特征检测器的性能，AUC最高提升7.0%，TPR@5%FPR最高提升15.0%。

Conclusion: CalibDCD作为一个通用的校准框架，有效地解决了后训练引起的成员特征偏移问题，证明了通过识别和校正系统性的特征偏移可以显著恢复并提升DCD方法在实际LLM场景中的检测能力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are trained on massive and largely undisclosed corpora that may contain copyrighted or privacy-sensitive content. Data contamination detection (DCD) therefore aims to determine whether a given text is a member of the pre-training corpus of a target LLM. Recent state-of-the-art DCD methods follow a feature-based paradigm that derives membership features from the input text and the corresponding model output. However, most modern LLMs undergo post-training, such as instruction tuning, preference optimization, and reasoning-oriented training, which can alter model outputs and shift the corresponding membership features, thereby reducing the separability between members and non-members.
  To address this problem, we propose CalibDCD, a broadly applicable calibration framework for feature-based DCD methods, comprising (1) Multi-View Shift Detection, which identifies recurring feature shifts associated with post-training, and (2) Bounded Feature Correction, which selectively mitigates their influence on membership prediction. Specifically, Multi-View Shift Detection evaluates controlled prompt variants on known non-member texts and consolidates the most informative views to identify recurring feature shifts. Bounded Feature Correction selectively adjusts feature components aligned with the detected shifts and controls the correction extent to preserve useful detection information.
  Experiments show that CalibDCD consistently improves existing feature-based detectors, with gains of up to 7.0% in AUC and 15.0% in TPR@5%FPR.

</details>


### [139] [Every Token Counts: Exact Likert-Scale Distributions for Measuring LLM Attitudes and Biases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10503)
*Davood Wadi,Mohsen Ghodrat,Matthew Philp*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个用于LLM受控行为评估的解析精确框架，通过全交叉因子实验、直接在token级概率质量函数上操作以及多元有序共识度量和分布ANOVA，将人类心理测量学与LLM机制相结合，以精确分离系统性偏见的因果来源，并通过消费者民族中心主义案例研究验证了该框架相对于传统聚合基准的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统NLP评估依赖大规模非结构化基准，这些数据集无法区分聚合偏见背后的因果机制——无法判断偏见来自模型基线特质、上下文混淆因素还是复杂交互作用。随着LLM被广泛部署为自主代理，准确评估其潜在价值和偏见变得至关重要，需要一种能够精确分离因果效应的受控评估方法。

Method: 该框架包含三个核心创新：(1) 用全交叉因子实验替代非结构化提示，系统隔离因果主效应和交互效应；(2) 消除蒙特卡洛文本采样噪声，直接在精确的token级概率质量函数（PMFs）上操作；(3) 推导出多元有序共识度量和分布ANOVA，以解析方式处理这些PMFs。通过将人类心理测量设计与LLM机制结合，弥补了设计、测量和分析三个环节的差距。

Result: 通过在五个LLM上进行消费者民族中心主义案例研究，验证了该框架能够隔离出聚合基准所掩盖的系统性国家原产地偏见，证明了该方法在揭示细粒度、可解释的模型偏差方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作为LLM受控行为评估提供了一个解析精确的理论框架，弥补了非结构化基准在因果归因上的根本缺陷，为理解和解构LLM潜在价值与偏见提供了更科学的方法论基础。

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed as autonomous agents, accurately evaluating their latent values and biases is critical. The NLP community typically evaluates models using large, unstructured benchmarks. While effective for general capabilities, these datasets fundamentally conflate causal mechanisms: even when an aggregate bias is detected, unstructured evaluations cannot disentangle whether it stems from baseline traits, contextual confounders, or complex interactions. To address this, we introduce an analytically exact framework for the controlled behavioral evaluation of LLMs. We bridge human psychometrics with LLM mechanics by resolving gaps in design, measurement, and analysis. First, we replace unstructured prompting with fully crossed factorial experiments to systematically isolate causal main and interaction effects. Second, we eliminate Monte Carlo text sampling noise by operating directly on exact, token-level Probability Mass Functions (PMFs). Third, we derive a multivariate ordinal consensus metric and a distributional ANOVA to process these PMFs analytically. We validate our framework with a case study on consumer ethnocentrism across five LLMs, demonstrating how our approach isolates systemic country-of-origin biases that aggregate benchmarks otherwise obscure.

</details>


### [140] [ASR-Roundtrip Evaluation Can Mask Context- and Convention-Dependent Reading Errors in Chinese News TTS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10606)
*Shijun Luo,Lizhi Wan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了ASR往返评估（ASR-roundtrip）在中文新闻TTS可懂度评估中的局限性，发现其对依赖上下文或领域约定的阅读错误存在"假阴性"问题。通过对110个高风险的MiMo TTS案例进行定向审计，确认了46个被掩盖的假阴性、9个暴露的TTS错误和55个无原始TTS错误的案例。跨度隔离诊断可重新暴露18/46个被掩盖的错误。在97个跨两次审计确认被掩盖的音频文件中，Qwen3-ASR可表面恢复40例，而Paraformer仅恢复2例。结论表明ASR往返评估适合作为筛查工具，但不足以作为中文新闻阅读风险评估的独立ground truth。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ASR往返评估（ASR-roundtrip）被广泛用作文本转语音（TTS）可懂度的可扩展代理指标，但该方法可能对听众能感知到的阅读错误产生假阴性结果。现有的评估体系过度依赖ASR往返作为质量评判标准，可能导致对TTS系统在特定高风险场景（如体育比分、飞机型号、技术单位和会员名称等规则性依赖的新闻文本）下的真实表现产生误判。作者希望通过系统性的定向审计揭示这一盲区，从而为TTS评估提供更可靠的基准。

Method: 研究选取中文新闻TTS片段，这些片段的正确阅读依赖于上下文或领域约定，涵盖体育比分、飞机型号、技术单位和会员名称等类别。对110个高风险的MiMo TTS案例进行定向审计（报告完整分母），并采用跨度隔离诊断（span-isolation diagnostic）方法重新检验被掩盖的错误。同时，在相同定向池中进行仅使用Raw（不含参考音频）的CosyVoice审计对照，并比较两种ASR系统（Qwen3-ASR与Paraformer）对确认被掩盖的错误案例的表面恢复能力。

Result: 在110个高风险MiMo案例中确认：46个被掩盖的假阴性错误、9个暴露的TTS错误和55个无Raw TTS错误的案例。跨度隔离诊断重新暴露了18/46个此前被掩盖的错误。Raw-only CosyVoice审计在同一池中确认了51个被掩盖的案例。在两次审计中共97个被确认掩盖的TTS特定音频文件中，Qwen3-ASR表面恢复了40例，而Paraformer仅恢复2例，显示不同ASR系统在掩盖错误恢复能力上存在显著差异。

Conclusion: ASR往返评估作为TTS可懂度的筛查工具是有价值的，但作为中文新闻阅读风险评估的独立ground truth是不充分的。其假阴性问题主要来自于ASR本身就具有上下文补全或表面校正的能力，会将被掩盖的错误自动纠正为预期文本。因此，需要结合人耳审计、跨度隔离诊断等多模态评估手段，才能对TTS在上下文依赖型文本上的阅读准确率进行可靠评估。

Abstract: ASR-roundtrip evaluation is widely used as a scalable proxy for text-to-speech (TTS) intelligibility, but it can produce false negatives for reading errors perceived by listeners. We study Chinese news TTS spans whose correct reading depends on context or domain conventions, such as sports scores, aircraft models, technical units, and membership names. In these cases, Raw TTS can choose a plausible but wrong reading while ASR transcribes the audio as the intended or surface-correct text. A targeted audit over 110 high-risk MiMo TTS cases, reported with a complete denominator, confirms 46 masked false negatives, 9 exposed TTS errors, and 55 cases with no Raw TTS error. A span-isolation diagnostic re-exposes 18/46 previously masked errors. A Raw-only CosyVoice audit on the same targeted pool confirms 51 masked cases. Across the 97 TTS-specific audio files labeled confirmed masked across the two audits, Qwen3-ASR surface-recovers 40 cases, whereas Paraformer does so in only 2. The results suggest that ASR-roundtrip is useful for screening but insufficient as standalone ground truth for Chinese news reading-risk evaluation.

</details>


### [141] [Simplex Relaxation for Discrete Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10615)
*Jinya Sakurai,Patrick Pynadath,Satoshi Hayakawa,Jaehong Yoon,Xulei Yang,Nancy F. Chen,Xun Xu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Simplax，一种精确的狄利克雷-分类增强方法，为均匀离散扩散模型引入辅助单纯形变量，在不改变底层分类损坏过程的前提下，获得可处理的Rao-Blackwellized逆向桥目标和随机逆向采样器，从而提升生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散扩散模型依赖损坏核定义中间状态空间和逆向预测问题。针对均匀离散扩散，作者想探究是否能在不改变底层分类损坏过程的条件下，丰富其训练目标和逆向转移，以提升生成性能。

Method: Simplax通过将每个损坏的分类状态与一个辅助单纯形值变量耦合，同时保持原始均匀扩散过程作为其分类边际。这一增强产生了可处理的Rao-Blackwellized逆向桥目标以及对应的随机逆向采样器，且保留损坏的分类状态作为去噪器的输入。

Result: 在无条件OpenWebText生成上改善了困惑度-熵权衡；在Sudoku任务中，仅用30条线索训练的模型在所有评估线索密度（包括最少唯一可解的17条线索）上达到最高准确率，在无条件生成中达到最高有效性。

Conclusion: Simplax证明通过精确的辅助变量增强可以显著提升均匀离散扩散模型的生成质量，且无需改变底层分类损坏过程，为离散扩散模型提供了一种实用且有效的改进方案。

Abstract: Discrete diffusion models for categorical generation are defined by a corruption kernel, which determines the intermediate state space and the associated reverse prediction problem. We study uniform discrete diffusion and ask whether its training objective and reverse transitions can be enriched without changing the underlying categorical corruption process. We introduce Simplax, an exact Dirichlet--categorical augmentation that couples each corrupted categorical state with an auxiliary simplex-valued variable while preserving the original uniform diffusion process as its categorical marginal. This augmentation yields a tractable Rao--Blackwellized reverse-bridge objective and a corresponding stochastic reverse sampler, while retaining the corrupted categorical state as the denoiser input. Empirically, Simplax improves the generative perplexity--entropy tradeoff on unconditional OpenWebText generation. On Sudoku, a model trained exclusively on $30$-clue puzzles achieves the highest accuracy among the compared methods across all evaluated clue densities, including the minimum uniquely solvable $17$-clue regime, and also achieves the highest validity in unconditional generation.

</details>


### [142] [Dual-Loop Self-Evolution via Verifiable Emotion Feedback for Multi-Turn Empathetic Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10626)
*Yi Wei,Shuo Jiang,Huaixia Dou,Jie Zhu,Junhui Li,Lifan Guo,Feng Chen,Chi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一个双循环自进化框架，利用可验证的情绪回报来训练多轮对话策略，解决了强化学习中策略能力与训练交互分布不匹配的问题，在SAGE基准上显著提升了共情能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型已具备对话能力，但在共情支持方面仍有挑战。共情支持本质上是一个多轮、路径依赖的过程：用户逐步披露问题、情绪随时间演变、早期回应塑造信任与接收度。强化学习虽然提供长程交互的可扩展监督，但现有方法在演化对话策略时保持训练交互分布固定，导致策略能力与训练经验之间出现错配。

Method: 提出双循环自我进化框架：用户模拟器和验证器冻结，内循环用连续情绪回报优化多轮策略，外循环用相同结果估计策略相关的交互效用并自适应经验分布。为从稀疏随机rollout中获得估计，框架在组内保持场景和交互状态不变，优先选择组通过率接近策略能力边界的条件。层级控制器在支持意图间共享证据，不确定性引导的探索和均匀排演防止过早排除，生成轨迹并闭合两循环且不增加rollout预算。

Result: 在SAGE基准上，该方法将Qwen3-8B的Overall从53.87提升到79.24，并超过协议匹配的均匀情绪回报强化学习7.23个点。

Conclusion: 通过将训练经验分布与策略能力对齐的双循环进化机制，可以在不增加rollout预算的情况下有效提升多轮共情对话能力，解决了RL训练中分布错配的瓶颈。

Abstract: Large language models have demonstrated conversational capabilities, yet empathetic competence remains challenging. Empathetic support is inherently multi-turn and path-dependent: users disclose concerns gradually, emotions evolve over time, and early responses shape trust and receptivity. Reinforcement learning with verifiable emotion rewards provides scalable supervision for long-horizon interactions. However, existing methods evolve the dialogue policy while keeping its training interaction distribution fixed, creating a mismatch between policy competence and training experience. We introduce a dual-loop self-evolution framework driven by verifiable emotion feedback. With the user simulator and verifier frozen, the inner loop optimizes the multi-turn policy using continuous emotion rewards, while the outer loop uses the same outcomes to estimate policy-relative interaction utility and adapt experience. To obtain estimates from sparse, stochastic rollouts, the framework holds the scenario and interaction state constant within each group and prioritizes conditions whose group pass rates lie near the policy's competence boundary. A hierarchical controller shares evidence across support intents, while uncertainty-guided exploration and uniform rehearsal prevent premature exclusion. The resulting distribution generates trajectories, closing both loops without increasing the rollout budget. On SAGE, our framework raises Qwen3-8B Overall from 53.87 to 79.24 and outperforms protocol-matched uniform emotion-reward reinforcement learning by 7.23 points.

</details>


### [143] [Decomposition-Induced Context-Memory Conflict: When Fact-Checking Pipelines Contradict Their Own Source Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10627)
*Yu-Feng Yen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文揭示"分解-验证"流水线中的分解器并非中性预处理步骤：分解器可被诱导用自身参数化信念替换源文本内容，产生的claim与原文矛盾，作者称之为"分解诱导的上下文-记忆冲突"(DI-CC)。该现象与经典上下文-记忆冲突机制相同，但发生在不同流水线阶段。线性探针可检测此现象(AUC 0.86-0.88)，现有无参考基线(SelfCheckGPT)完全失效(AUC 0.51，随机水平)，而上下文感知解码虽可缓解但代价过高，尚不可部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究将分解(Decomposition)视为中立的预处理步骤，但作者怀疑分解器并非中立——它可能用自己的参数化记忆替代源文本内容，产生与原文矛盾的claims。这一现象存在于分解-验证流水线的早期阶段，尚未被系统性研究，且已有的检测与缓解方法是否适用未知。

Method: 作者定义并形式化DI-CC现象，将其与经典上下文-记忆冲突在机制上统一。使用仅基于经典冲突数据(NQ-Swap)训练的线性探针来分离DI-CC位置与忠实分解位置；对比SelfCheckGPT自一致性采样基线；并测试上下文感知解码作为迁移缓解手段。

Result: 线性探针在未见分解数据上达到AUC 0.86-0.88(置换检验p<0.0005)；SelfCheckGPT无法检测DI-CC(AUC 0.51，随机水平)因为DI-CC内容稳定可复现、在重采样间重复出现；上下文感知解码可抑制DI-CC但在指代密集条件下导致大量分解解析失败，常因分解器虚构不同身份所致；该现象自然发生率稀疏、需最小模型规模才能建立，作者认为其影响范围不应被夸大。

Conclusion: DI-CC是真实、机制上有据可依且部分可缓解的失效模式，但作者明确表示缓解方案尚未达到可部署标准，且该现象的scope需要审慎评估，不应过度夸大其影响。

Abstract: Decompose-then-verify pipelines, including FActScore-style fact-checkers and long-form factuality evaluators, first split a passage into atomic claims before checking each one. Decomposition itself is treated as a neutral preprocessing step. We show it is not: a decomposer can be induced to substitute its own parametric belief for what the source passage says, producing a claim that contradicts the text it was supposed to summarize faithfully. We call this Decomposition-Induced Context-Memory Conflict (DI-CC) and show it is mechanistically the same phenomenon as classical context-memory conflict, occurring inside a different pipeline stage than prior work has examined. A linear probe trained only on classical context-memory conflict data (NQ-Swap), never exposed to any decomposition output, significantly separates decomposition positions that produce DI-CC from faithful decompositions (AUC = 0.86-0.88, permutation p < 0.0005). An existing reference-free baseline, SelfCheckGPT-style self-consistency sampling, fails to detect DI-CC at all (AUC 0.51, chance-level), because DI-CC content is stably recoverable and recurs across resamples, unlike the variability self-consistency methods rely on. Context-aware decoding, a training-free mitigation from the classical setting, transfers to decomposition and suppresses DI-CC, but at a severe cost: many decompositions under coreference-heavy conditions fail to parse, often because the decomposer fabricates a different identity. We do not consider this mitigation deployment-ready. We further characterize the mechanism's boundaries: its natural occurrence rate is too sparss not manifest on naturally-occurring hallucinatedtext, and it requires a minimum model scale to detecablish DI-CC as a real, mechanistically grounded, andpartially treatable failure mode, with a scope we chhan overstate.

</details>


### [144] [Seeds Before Objectives: Rethinking Evaluation for Low-Resource Garhwali ASR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10670)
*Karamvir Singh Batra,Prathamjyot Singh,Ashima Sood,Jasmeet Singh,Sahil Sharma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文构建了首个基于官方VAANI划分的可复现多种子（multi-seed）加瓦尔语（Garhwali）自动语音识别（ASR）基准，通过种子级显著性检验发现Focal CTC、matra加权目标函数等看似有希望的改进方案实属脆弱，而标准CTC配上w2v-BERT 2.0预训练模型在五粒种子下达到47.0%的词错误率（WER），证明多种子评估能将真实收益与种子噪声分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低资源方言在小语料规模下，单次运行（single-run）的对比实验结果往往无法复现，这导致研究结论不可靠。为加瓦尔语（一种印度-雅利安低资源语言）建立可靠的基准，需要突破传统单次实验的局限，通过可复现的多种子实验和统计检验来甄别真实的性能提升。

Method: 在官方VAANI划分上构建首个可复现的多种子ASR基准，具体做法包括：对同一设定运行多粒随机种子并输出逐种子结果，采用种子级显著性检验来评估差异；系统性地重新检验Focal CTC、matra加权目标函数以及Hindi-to-Garhwali迁移学习等候选优化方案。

Result: 实验结果显示，Focal CTC和matra加权目标函数在种子级检验下均未能优于标准CTC，matra目标甚至未能降低其目标错误类型；Hindi到Garhwali的迁移学习相比直接微调无收益。而标准CTC配合w2v-BERT 2.0达到47.0% WER（平均五粒种子），优于更大的MMS-1B及同类模型。预训练设计而非参数规模驱动性能，速度增强（speed augmentation）带来小而基本一致的收益。

Conclusion: 在低资源方言ASR中，多种子评估和官方数据划分上的显著性检验是区分真实改进与随机波动的关键方法。看似新颖的复杂方案往往不敌坚实的预训练模型设计配合标准目标函数，研究应回归可复现的实验基准。

Abstract: At corpus sizes typical of low-resource dialects, single-run comparisons can yield gains that do not replicate. We show this for Garhwali, an under-resourced Indo-Aryan language of the central Himalaya, building the first reproducible multi-seed ASR benchmark on the official VAANI splits, with per-seed outputs and significance testing. Re-examining plausible gains, we find them fragile: neither Focal CTC nor a matra-weighted objective beats standard CTC under seed-level testing, the matra objective fails to cut even its targeted errors, and Hindi-to-Garhwali transfer gives no gain over direct fine-tuning. What holds up is mundane: w2v-BERT 2.0 with standard CTC reaches 47.0% WER over five seeds, beating the larger MMS-1B and comparable models; pretraining design, not parameter count, drives performance, and speed augmentation gives a small, largely consistent gain. Multi-seed evaluation on official splits separates real gains from seed noise.

</details>


### [145] [Auditing Chinese Web-scale Corpora via Sampled BPE Token Statistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10678)
*Qingjie Zhang,Ziqi Tang,Jie Zhang,Gelei Deng,Jinfeng Li,YueFeng Chen,Yitong Yang,Hui Xue,Tianwei Zhang,Han Qiu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Sampled-BPE，一种轻量级token级别的审计管道，通过采样小规模子集并训练BPE分词器来检测中文预训练语料中的网络污染。实验表明该方法在保持可用估计精度的同时，实现了148.4倍加速和35.8倍内存缩减（相对误差仅4.25%）。作者将管道应用于11个开源中文语料库和2021至2026年的6个中文Common Crawl快照，揭示了开源语料中广泛但不均匀的污染，以及高度污染且随时间变化的中文网络内容。文章还发布了一个包含66万+条token记录的分层中文网页token数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中文网络污染（如垃圾信息、低质量内容）已渗透到大语言模型中，但审计上游中文语料面临三大挑战：(1) 语料规模达web级，全量扫描成本过高；(2) 以往分析粒度太粗，无法暴露token级别的污染；(3) 中文网络污染具有隐性和快速变化的特点。因此需要一种高效、细粒度的审计方法。

Method: 提出Sampled-BPE管道：从大规模中文语料中采样小子集，训练BPE tokenizer，利用分词结果将污染暴露在token级别。管道包含采样、BPE训练、污染token检测与分类等环节，并通过领域知识构建层级化的token组织方式以支持污染溯源与审查。

Result: Sampled-BPE相比全量扫描实现148.4倍加速和35.8倍内存降低，污染类别估计的相对误差仅为4.25%。应用该管道审计11个开源中文语料库和6个中文Common Crawl快照（2021-2026），发现开源语料中污染广泛但不均匀分布，中文网络内容污染严重且随时间动态变化。发布了包含66万+token记录的分层中文web token数据集。

Conclusion: Sampled-BPE为大规模中文语料污染审计提供了一种高效、可扩展的token级解决方案，验证了采样+BPE分词在污染检测中的有效性。研究揭示了中文开源语料和网络内容中污染问题的严重性与时空分布特征，所发布的数据集支持后续的污染审查、追踪与治理工作。

Abstract: Chinese web pollution has surfaced in LLMs, motivating audits of upstream Chinese corpora. However, auditing such corpora faces three challenges: (1) their web-scale size makes full scan costly; (2) prior analyses are often too coarse to expose token-level pollution; (3) Chinese web pollution is implicit and rapidly changing. We propose Sampled-BPE, a lightweight token-level auditing pipeline that sample a small subset and train BPE tokenizer to surface polluted tokens. Experiments show that Sampled-BPE preserves usable estimates while substantially reducing runtime and memory: a 148.4 $\times$ speedup and a 35.8 $\times$ memory reduction induce only 4.25% relative error for pollution categories. We apply the pipeline to 11 open Chinese corpora and 6 Chinese Common Crawl snapshots from 2021 to 2026. The audit reveals widespread but uneven pollution across open corpora, as well as highly polluted and temporally shifting Chinese web content. We further release a hierarchical Chinese web token dataset with 660k+ token records, each with web context, category, and explanation fields, organized as trees to support review and tracing of pollution.

</details>


### [146] [Leveraging Human Reading Behavior for Keyphrase Extraction: A Webcam-based Eye-tracking Corpus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10688)
*Chengzhi Zhang,Xinyi Yan,Wenqi Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了基于网络摄像头（webcam）的轻量级眼动追踪特征能否提升中文图书情报学（LIS）学术摘要的关键词抽取（KPE）性能。作者开发了利用开源SearchGazer库的数据采集平台，构建了中文LIS眼动追踪语料库（CLIS-ET），并将首视注视时长（FFD）、注视次数（FN）和总注视时长（TFD）三个字符级眼动特征融入KPE模型。实验结果表明，眼动特征一致性地提升了KPE性能，其中FN与TFD的组合在Att-BiLSTM+CRF模型上取得最佳效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关键词抽取研究主要关注改进文本表示，而很大程度上忽视了人类阅读行为这一重要信号。眼睛注视行为在阅读中能反映读者的注意力分配和语义理解，有望为关键词识别提供补充信息。但中文阅读领域的眼动数据匮乏，且传统眼动设备成本高昂、操作复杂，限制了该方向的研究。

Method: 利用开源的SearchGazer库构建基于网络摄像头的轻量级数据采集平台（成本低、易部署），采集读者阅读中文LIS学术摘要时的眼动数据，构建CLIS-ET语料库。提取三个字符级眼动特征：FFD、FN和TFD，将其作为额外的特征输入融入关键词抽取模型（如Att-BiLSTM+CRF），通过对比实验评估眼动特征对KPE性能的贡献。

Result: 眼动特征一致性地改善了KPE性能。在Att-BiLSTM+CRF模型上，FN与TFD的组合取得了最佳成绩，表明读者的注视行为能够为识别学术摘要中的关键词提供有效信号。

Conclusion: 该研究验证了低成本网络摄像头眼动追踪技术在KPE任务中的可行性与价值，表明将人类阅读行为融入关键词抽取能够有效提升性能。同时贡献了首个包含FFD、FN、TFD特征的中文学术眼动语料库CLIS-ET以及相关数据集和代码。

Abstract: Purpose: Keyphrases are statistically and semantically important textual units that can also attract readers' attention during comprehension. However, existing keyphrase extraction (KPE) studies mainly focus on improving textual representation while largely overlooking human reading behavior. This study examines whether lightweight webcam-based eye-tracking features can improve KPE from Chinese academic abstracts in Library and Information Science (LIS).
  Methodology: To address the limited availability of eye-tracking data for Chinese academic reading, we developed a lightweight webcam-based data collection platform using the open-source SearchGazer library and constructed the Chinese LIS Eye-Tracking Corpus (CLIS-ET). Three character-level eye-tracking features, first fixation duration (FFD), fixation number (FN), and total fixation duration (TFD), were incorporated into KPE models to evaluate their effects on extraction performance.
  Findings: Eye-tracking features consistently improved KPE performance. The combination of FN and TFD achieved the best results on the Att-BiLSTM+CRF model, indicating that readers' fixation behavior provides useful signals for identifying keyphrases in academic abstracts.
  Originality/value: This study introduces a cost-effective webcam-based eye-tracking approach for KPE and presents CLIS-ET, a Chinese academic eye-tracking corpus containing FFD, FN, and TFD features. The results demonstrate the value of incorporating human reading behavior into keyphrase extraction. Dataset and code: https://github.com/yan-xinyi/ET_AKE and https://github.com/yan-xinyi/Reading_ET_System.

</details>


### [147] [Can Released LLM Vocabularies Support Token-Level Estimation of Hidden Corpora?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10690)
*Qingjie Zhang,Xingzhang Ren,Zixuan Chen,Jinfeng Li,YueFeng Chen,Yitong Yang,Hui Xue,Dayiheng Liu,Han Qiu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种从已发布的分词器词表中估计任意目标词元在预训练语料中占比的方法（QGDE），通过在不同语料训练的BPE分词器间迁移稳定的词元ID-占比分布，实现细粒度语料组成估计，词元级别平均相对误差低至3.00%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练语料组成在很大程度上决定了大语言模型的能力，但即使模型权重公开，语料组成信息往往仍然隐藏。先前工作要么只能推断语料混合比例，要么只能追踪特定词元组，无法对任意目标词元进行占比估计。作者希望利用已发布的分词器词汇表这一信号，实现任意目标词元的细粒度语料占比估计。

Method: 首先通过实验证明在不同语料上训练的BPE分词器之间，词元ID与语料占比的分布是稳定的，从而支持从已知语料向目标（隐藏）分词器进行分布迁移。在此基础上提出Quantile-Guided Density Estimation（QGDE）：该方法用多个分位数趋势近似该分布，并通过局部密度加权产生词元级别的占比估计。

Result: 在受控实验和基于已发布SmolLM分词器的现实场景中，QGDE在词元级别的平均相对误差低至3.00%，在聚合为类别级别的语料混合后误差为3.08%，显著优于粗粒度组成推断。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，已发布的分词器词汇表为细粒度语料估计提供了有价值的信号，超越了以往仅能做粗粒度组成的推断水平，为语言模型预训练语料组成的量化分析提供了新途径。

Abstract: Pretraining corpus composition shapes LLM capabilities, but it often remains hidden even when model weights are released. Prior work has inferred corpus mixtures or traced specific token groups from released tokenizer vocabularies; in contrast, we estimate corpus ratios for arbitrary target tokens. We first show that BPE tokenizers trained on different corpora share stable token ID--ratio distributions, motivating distribution transfer from known corpora to a target tokenizer trained on hidden corpora. We then propose Quantile-Guided Density Estimation (QGDE), which approximates this distribution with multiple quantile trends and uses local density weighting to produce token-level estimates. In controlled settings and a realistic setting using the released SmolLM tokenizer, QGDE achieves mean relative errors as low as 3.00% for token-level estimation and 3.08% after aggregation into category-level mixtures. These results suggest that released tokenizer vocabularies provide a useful signal for fine-grained corpus estimation beyond coarse composition inference.

</details>


### [148] [SPIEval: Evaluating Large Language Models as Mobile Assistants over Scattered Personal Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10692)
*Junjie Ye,Zhuohui Sheng,Shaofan Liu,Yulun Zhu,Wenjie Fu,Dingwei Zhu,Ming Zhang,Yujiong Shen,Weichao Wang,Xin Zhao,Shihan Dou,Tao Gui,Qi Zhang,Xuanjing Huang,Pluto Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SPIEval，一个基于人类专家构建的基准测试，用于评估大语言模型作为移动助手时跨多应用整合个人信息的能力。该基准涵盖五大认知能力、250个任务和4335条个人记录，横跨10个应用并支持21个工具的多轮交互。评估9个代表性LLM后发现最佳模型仅达57.3%准确率，最差仅16.4%，且79%的失败源于信息定位不准确，凸显了当前LLM移动助手在个人信息利用上的根本局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM越来越多地被部署为移动助手，关键挑战在于需跨多个应用利用分散的个人信息来完成用户指令，但缺乏专门的基准来评估其这一能力，因此其能力表现尚不明确。

Method: 构建了SPIEval基准，其设计基于五大认知能力（推理、消歧、整合、偏好推断和多意图分解），包含250个任务、4335条个人记录、10个应用和21个工具，支持多轮交互。该基准具备任务场景多样、任务具有挑战性、信息分散、环境可控和结果可验证等特点。

Result: 评估9个代表性LLM，最佳模型GPT-5.5(xhigh)仅达57.3%准确率，最弱模型仅16.4%。进一步分析发现79%的失败源于不准确的信息定位，LLM常固守看似合理但不正确的信息而不继续检索验证；且仅不到2%的检索动作使用高级搜索方法，模型间搜索效率差异显著。

Conclusion: 当前基于LLM的移动助手在个人信息整合利用方面存在根本性局限，尤其在信息定位和检索策略上表现不足。这些发现为该领域未来的研究指明了方向，相关数据和代码已公开。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed as mobile assistants, where a key challenge is leveraging personal information scattered across multiple applications (apps) to complete user instructions. However, due to the lack of dedicated benchmarks, their capabilities remain poorly understood. To address this gap, we introduce SPIEval, a human-curated benchmark grounded in five cognitive capabilities (i.e., reasoning, disambiguation, integration, preference inference, and multi-intent decomposition). SPIEval comprises 250 tasks spanning 4,335 personal records distributed across 10 apps and supports multi-turn interaction through 21 tools. Analysis shows that the benchmark exhibits diverse scenarios, challenging tasks, scattered information, controllable environments, and verifiable outcomes. We evaluate nine representative LLMs and find substantial room for improvement. The best-performing model, GPT-5.5 (xhigh), achieves only 57.3% accuracy, while the weakest achieves just 16.4%. Further analysis reveals that 79% of failures stem from inaccurate information localization, as LLMs often commit to plausible but incorrect information instead of continuing retrieval for verification. We also find that fewer than 2% of retrieval actions employ advanced search methods and observe substantial variation in search efficiency across models. These findings expose fundamental limitations of current LLM-based mobile assistants and motivate future research in this direction. Data and code are available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/Junjie-Ye/SPIEval.

</details>


### [149] [EVIL-Detect for NLPCC 2026 Shared Task 6: LLM-Generated Text Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10698)
*Hongrui Bao,Hangyu Rong,Zhuoshang Wang,Yubing Ren,Yanan Cao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了EVIL-Detect，一个用于NLPCC 2026 Shared Task 6的多信号集成框架，通过冲突感知融合机制检测LLM生成文本，在中文场景下（HWT、LGT、HLT三类）以0.8888的macro-F1分数排名官方评测第一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型的快速发展，可靠检测LLM生成文本的需求日益迫切，尤其是在中文现实场景中涉及人类撰写文本（HWT）、LLM生成文本（LGT）和LLM改写文本（HLT）的区分任务，需要更鲁棒的检测方法以应对分布偏移。

Method: 提出多信号集成框架EVIL-Detect，采用冲突感知融合策略，整合四种信号：编辑程度回归（edit-extent regression）、零样本似然对比信号（zero-shot likelihood-contrast signals）、词汇统计特征（lexical statistics）以及保守文本规则（conservative text rules），并通过校准的决策边界进行冲突感知集成。

Result: 系统在强分布外偏移下展现出较高鲁棒性，取得0.8888的macro-F1分数，在NLPCC 2026 Shared Task 6官方评测中排名第一。

Conclusion: 冲突感知融合策略与多信号集成的组合能有效提升LLM文本检测在中文现实场景下的鲁棒性和性能，该方法的代码已开源供进一步研究使用。

Abstract: The rapid development of large language models (LLMs) has increased the need for reliable detection of LLM-generated text, especially in realistic Chinese scenarios involving human-written text (HWT), LLM-generated text (LGT), and LLM-refined text (HLT). This paper presents EVIL-Detect, a multi-signal ensemble framework with conflict-aware fusion for NLPCC 2026 Shared Task 6. The system integrates edit-extent regression, zero-shot likelihood-contrast signals, lexical statistics, and conservative text rules. With calibrated decision boundaries and conflict-aware integration, our system improves robustness under strong out-of-distribution shifts, achieving a macro-F1 score of 0.8888 and ranking first in the official evaluation. Our code is available at https://github.com/bbbbhrrrr/evildetect.

</details>


### [150] [Most biomedical publications show signs of LLM-assisted writing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10715)
*Lena Holzwarth,Rita González-Márquez,Dmitry Kobak*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于词频变化的无偏估计方法，用于检测学术出版物中LLM辅助写作的使用比例。将该方法应用于PubMed Central的开放获取生物医学论文全文，发现到2025年底，89%的论文存在LLM相关词汇的过量使用；Discussion段落中使用LLM的比例（68%）是Methods段落（32%）的两倍，且Methods段落内部LLM使用率也超过50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM驱动的聊天机器人和智能体已被广泛用于学术写作，虽然能消除语言障碍，但也引发学术不端和欺诈的担忧。为了为政策制定提供依据，需要监测学术出版物中LLM改写文本的普遍程度，但现有方法无法产生可靠估计，因此亟需一种无偏、可靠的检测方法。

Method: 提出了一种基于语料库中词频变化的新颖无偏估计方法。该方法通过检测LLM关联词汇在文本中的分布变化来估计LLM使用比例，并应用于PubMed Central的开放获取生物医学论文全文语料。

Result: 到2025年底，89%的论文显示出LLM关联词汇的过量使用。Discussion段落中LLM使用概率（68%）是Methods段落（32%）的两倍，但Methods段落内部LLM总体使用率仍超过50%。

Conclusion: 该估计对于制定未来的学术写作指南和政策至关重要，为监测LLM在学术出版物中的使用提供了可靠依据。

Abstract: Over the past several years, LLM-powered chatbots and agents have become widely used as a tool for academic writing. LLM-assisted writing can be valuable by removing language barriers but at the same time causes concerns about misconduct and fraud. To inform policy decisions, it is necessary to monitor the prevalence of LLM-altered texts in scholarly publications. Despite some recent progress in this direction, no existing method can produce reliable estimates. Here we suggest and validate a new unbiased approach to estimate LLM usage in a corpus of texts based on changing word frequencies. We apply our method to the full texts of open-access biomedical papers from Pubmed Central, and show that by the end of 2025, 89% of papers show excess of LLM-associated vocabulary. We also find that LLMs are twice as likely to be used when writing a paragraph in the Discussion section (68%) compared to a paragraph in the Methods section (32%), but even inside the Methods section, the overall prevalence of LLM usage is over 50%. We believe that our estimates are crucial to shape future guidelines and policies.

</details>


### [151] [Mitigating Context Interference for Reliable and Efficient Search Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10743)
*Boyang Xue,Bin Wu,Shuofei Qiao,Sheng Wang,Rui Wang,Yiming Du,Hongru Wang,Jeff Z. Pan,Emine Yilmaz,Kam-Fai Wong,Aldo Lipani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了多轮搜索智能体中LLM面临的"上下文干扰"问题，发现干扰主要来源于最新检索的文档，并提出基于蒸馏的上下文精炼器来动态缓解干扰，最后验证了将上下文精炼融入RL训练流水线可显著提升搜索智能体的可靠性和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轮搜索智能体的上下文冗长复杂，每轮检索引入的文档不可避免地包含不相关信息，造成"上下文干扰"，损害LLM搜索智能体的可靠性和效率。作者希望系统性地理解这种干扰的来源、缓解方法以及是否能在训练阶段进一步优化。

Method: 1) 系统性地探究上下文干扰的三方面问题：干扰来自上下文的哪些部分、如何精炼上下文以缓解干扰、将上下文精炼融入搜索智能体训练能否进一步改进。2) 基于研究发现（干扰主要源于最新检索的文档），引入一个基于蒸馏（distill-based）的上下文精炼器，动态缓解上下文干扰。3) 将上下文精炼集成进搜索智能体的RL训练流水线中。

Result: 实验验证表明：干扰主要来自最新检索的文档；提出的蒸馏式上下文精炼器能有效动态缓解上下文干扰；将上下文精炼融入RL训练流水线可显著提升搜索智能体的可靠性和效率。

Conclusion: 该研究凸显了缓解搜索智能体上下文干扰的重要性，启发了"先精炼上下文再生成"（refine context and then generate）的AI智能体新范式。

Abstract: Recent research empowers Large Language Models (LLMs) as multi-turn search agents to iteratively retrieve and generate outputs until complex tasks are solved. However, the contexts of multi-turn search agents are lengthy and complex. For example, the retrieved set of documents in each turn would inevitably introduce irrelevant information that distracts LLMs, referring to \textit{context interference}, potentially hindering the reliability and efficiency of search agents. Therefore, we conduct a systematic study on context interference in multi-turn search agents, focusing on investigating i) which parts of the context of search agents will contribute to the context interference, ii) how to refine the contexts of search agents to mitigate the interference, and iii) can incorporating context refinement into search agent training yield further improvements. We reveal that interference primarily arises from the latest retrieved documents. Based on the explored findings, we then introduce a distill-based context refiner to dynamically mitigate context interference for multi-turn search agents. Finally, we validate that incorporating context refinement into RL training pipelines of search agents can significantly enhance both reliability and efficiency. This study highlights the importance of mitigating context interference of search agents, inspiring a novel paradigm of ``refine context and then generate'' for AI agents.

</details>


### [152] [Assessing Reliability of BERT-Based Models on Question Answering Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10806)
*Pooja Yadav,Priyanka Harjule,Basant Agarwal,Marko Robnik Šikonja*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究评估了四种基于BERT的问答模型（BERT、RoBERTa、ALBERT、DistilBERT）在SQuAD和QuAC数据集上的可靠性，通过Monte Carlo Dropout（MCD）引起的内部模型变化和释义引起的输入扰动来评估响应稳定性。结果表明RoBERTa保持更高的可靠性，而ALBERT和DistilBERT表现出显著的不一致性，且统计验证了MCD作为可靠性指标的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型的可靠性在许多情况下与准确性同等重要，因为可靠的模型更值得信赖、更稳健、更适合实际应用。然而，尽管基于BERT的编码器预训练Transformer在问答分类任务中表现出显著的准确性，其可靠性仍未被充分探索，因此需要系统性地评估这些模型在预测中的稳定性。

Method: 采用两种可靠性评估条件：(1)通过Monte Carlo Dropout（MCD）引入内部模型变化，改变dropout率以评估预测一致性；(2)通过释义进行输入扰动，检验词汇变化对答案稳定性的影响。使用SQuAD和QuAC数据集，对四种BERT变体模型（BERT、RoBERTa、ALBERT、DistilBERT）进行系统性评估，并通过统计分析验证MCD在预测时不会干扰推理动态。

Result: 实验发现RoBERTa在可靠性方面表现最优，而ALBERT和DistilBERT则表现出显著的不一致性。统计分析证实启用MCD进行预测不会破坏推理动态，验证了其作为可靠性度量指标的有效性。

Conclusion: 研究强调了在QA模型中同时评估准确性和稳定性（可靠性）的重要性，以确保模型在实际应用中保持稳定表现。这些发现为选择更可靠的问答模型和评估模型可靠性的方法论提供了指导。

Abstract: Reliability estimation of large language models is in many cases as crucial as their accuracy, as reliable models are more trustworthy, robust, and suitable for practical applications. Recent advancements in natural language processing (NLP), particularly those based on transformer architectures, have significantly accelerated progress across various NLP tasks. This study focuses on the reliability of transformer-based question answering (QA) models, specifically BERT models and its variants (RoBERTa, ALBERT, DistilBERT). These encoder-only pretrained transformers have demonstrated remarkable accuracy in QA tasks that can be treated as classification tasks. However, their reliability remains underexplored. This study evaluates the reliability of four BERT-based models by assessing response stability under two conditions: (1) internal model variations induced via Monte Carlo Dropout (MCD) and (2) input perturbations through paraphrasing. Using the SQuAD and QuAC datasets, we investigate how dropout rates affect prediction consistency and whether lexical changes impact answer stability. Our findings reveal that RoBERTa maintains higher reliability, whereas AlBERT and DistilBERT exhibit significant inconsistencies. Statistical analyses confirm that enabling MCD during prediction does not disrupt inference dynamics, validating its effectiveness as a reliability metric. These findings underscore the importance of evaluating both accuracy and stability in QA models to ensure stability in real-world applications.

</details>


### [153] [Surfacing the Unsaid: CUE-Bench for Affective Stance in Chinese Discourse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10810)
*Zhenyan Zheng,Yunyao Zhang,Junxi Sheng,Junqing Yu,Zikai Song*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出CUE Bench，一个中文未言说情感基准，聚焦情感立场（Affective Stance），通过显式/隐式极性交互构建九种人类可解释的情感立场，并提供意图和细粒度情感标注，以解决现有情感基准无法捕捉隐式、讽刺、礼貌等深层情感理解的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有情感基准主要标注表层极性或最终情感类别，缺乏对显式表达、隐式情感、语用意图和细粒度情感之间结构化交互的刻画，导致对隐含、弱化、反转或语用重塑的情感意义评估不敏感，掩盖了模型在深层情感理解上的失败。

Method: 引入CUE Bench，一个以情感立场为核心的中文未言说情感基准。从显式-隐式极性交互中构建九种人类可解释的情感立场，并额外提供意图标注和细粒度情感标注，以支持结构化情感推理。

Result: 实验表明，融入情感立场相比强基线，细粒度情感识别提升3.5个百分点，语用意图检测提升7.8个百分点。

Conclusion: 情感立场作为中间结构可以有效增强模型的深层情感理解能力，CUE Bench为衡量和提升隐式、间接情感推理提供了更全面的评估基准。

Abstract: Emotion understanding in discourse requires reasoning beyond surface sentiment because speakers often convey affect through indirect, implicit, polite, ironic, or deliberately mismatched expressions. Existing emotion benchmarks mainly annotate surface polarity or final emotion categories, while lacking a structured account of how explicit expression, implicit affect, pragmatic intent, and fine grained emotion interact. This limitation makes current evaluations insensitive to cases where affective meaning is concealed, weakened, inverted, or pragmatically reshaped, thereby obscuring model failures in deeper emotion understanding. To address this gap, we introduce CUE Bench, a Chinese Unsaid Emotion benchmark that centers on Affective Stance and covers diverse communicative scenarios. CUE Bench constructs nine human interpretable affective stances from explicit implicit polarity interaction and further provides intent and fine grained emotion annotations for structured affective inference. Experiments show that incorporating Affective Stance improves fine grained emotion recognition by 3.5 percentage points and pragmatic intent detection by 7.8 percentage points over strong baselines.

</details>


### [154] [Reference-Free Post-Training of Open Large Language Models for Multilingual Machine Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10812)
*Chris Han,Pengzhi Gao,Pei Fu,Jian Luan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MiLMMT-46-v1.0模型，通过GRPO强化学习（以参考无关的QE模型评分与语言识别门控相结合作为奖励）+ 线性插值SFT与RL检查点，在46种语言的多语言机器翻译上超越Seed-X、HY-MT2、TranslateGemma等强基线，并取得领先的参考无关分数，接近甚至超越Google Translate、Gemini 3 Pro和GPT-5等专有系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索无需参考译本的强化学习微调方法（reference-free post-training）用于多语言机器翻译，避免依赖昂贵且难获取的人工参考注释，同时利用开放大语言模型提升翻译质量。

Method: 1) 基于SFT模型MiLMMT-46-v0.1；2) 使用GRPO算法，奖励为两个参考无关质量估计模型的平均值，并通过语言识别（LID）进行门控；3) 将SFT与RL检查点做线性插值得到最终模型MiLMMT-46-v1.0；4) 另探究on-policy蒸馏，但发现其仅达到RL+检查点插值的质量边界而未超越。

Result: 在46种语言上一致超越SFT基线，优于Seed-X、HY-MT2、TranslateGemma等最新开源基线；在参考无关评估指标上领先于Google Translate、Gemini 3 Pro、GPT-5等专有系统。

Conclusion: 参考无关的GRPO强化学习配合检查点线性插值能有效提升多语言MT质量，是超越专有系统的可行路线；on-policy蒸馏虽有效但未突破同一质量上限。模型与代码已开源。

Abstract: We study reference-free post-training for multilingual machine translation with open large language models. Starting from the supervised-finetuned MiLMMT-46-v0.1 models, we apply Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) with a reward that averages two reference-free quality estimation models and is gated by language identification. We then linearly interpolate the supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and reinforcement learning (RL) model checkpoints to obtain MiLMMT-46-v1.0. Across 46 languages, the resulting models consistently improve translation quality over their SFT counterparts, outperform strong recent open baselines, including Seed-X, HY-MT2, and TranslateGemma, and achieve leading reference-free scores against evaluated proprietary systems such as Google Translate, Gemini 3 Pro, and GPT-5. We further investigate on-policy distillation and find that it reaches, but does not surpass, the quality frontier achieved by RL with checkpoint interpolation. We release the models and code to facilitate future research.

</details>


### [155] [VibeLifeBench: Can Your Life Agent Be Proactive and Persistent in a Living World?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10875)
*Xiaohongshu Inc*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: VibeLifeBench是一个包含200个跨十个日常生活领域的长时程任务基准，模拟22个mock服务的多周时间线世界。该基准衡量代理在未被打扰时主动发现世界变化、保持计划一致性的能力，并通过细粒度加权检查评分。七个前沿模型在该基准上均得分很低，表明当前代理与实际生活辅助之间仍有巨大差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM代理评估大多使用短小、自包含的请求和静态环境，而现实生活中的助手任务通常是持续数周、环境动态变化、约束往往未曾明说的。这需要代理具备主动性（proactive）和一致性（consistent），而当前没有任何基准能够衡量这种能力。作者旨在填补这一评估空白。

Method: 构建VibeLifeBench基准：包含200个长时程任务，覆盖十个日常生活领域；每个任务是在22个mock服务组成的模拟世界中运行的脚本化多周时间线；世界以自身时钟推进，许多变化是静默的（silent），只有主动重新检查世界的代理才能发现；评分采用细粒度加权检查，仅依据代理实际留下的行为记录，涵盖最终状态、行动及时性以及是否遵守隐含约束。

Result: 作者评估了七个前沿模型，所有模型在VibeLifeBench上均得分很低，表明当前代理距离真正的生活辅助能力仍有很大差距。

Conclusion: VibeLifeBench揭示了现有LLM代理在长期、动态、隐含约束的现实生活场景中的显著不足，并为该方向的研究提供了开源的任务集、环境和评估框架，以推动代理从被动响应向主动、持续的生活助手能力发展。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly deployed as personal assistants. Existing evaluations, however, mostly use short, self-contained requests in static environments. Everyday life assistance is different. A task runs for weeks rather than minutes. The world keeps changing while the agent is not being prompted. Many constraints are never stated outright. An agent that merely answers the request in front of it will fail at such a task. What is needed instead is an agent that stays proactive and consistent. It decides on its own when to act, when to ask, and when to stay silent. It notices changes that nobody announced. It keeps one plan coherent from the first day to the last. No current benchmark measures this. We introduce VibeLifeBench, a benchmark of 200 long-horizon tasks across ten everyday-life domains. Each task is a scripted multi-week timeline in a simulated world of 22 mock services. The world advances on its own clock, and many of its changes are silent, so only an agent that re-inspects the world discovers them. Every task is graded by fine-grained, weighted checks that read only what the agent actually left behind, covering the end state, the timeliness of its actions, and whether it upheld the implicit constraints. We evaluate seven frontier models. All of them score low, which shows how far current agents are from assisting with real life. We will open-source all tasks, environments, and the evaluation framework.

</details>


### [156] [X2-Turn: Frame-Synchronous Dual-Head Modeling for Joint Streaming ASR and Turn State Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10878)
*Kaiqi Fu,Rime Wen,Altman Lin,Shawn Qin,Roy Gan,Hao Wang,Qian Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出X2-Turn，一种基于延迟流建模的帧同步轮转状态预测方法，在预训练Voxtral Realtime模型上并行添加帧级轮转状态头与ASR头，实现低延迟、高精度的中英双语对话轮转检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模块化方法通常在话语级或固定块级优化轮转状态预测，与实际需要的连续轮转状态估计不匹配；同时依赖辅助ASR模型，限制响应速度并增加系统复杂度。需要一种帧同步、无需额外ASR模型的轮转状态预测方案。

Method: 基于预训练的Voxtral Realtime模型，引入帧同步轮转状态头（frame-synchronous turn state head），与ASR头并行工作在共享流式表示上，通过延迟流建模（delayed-stream modeling）联合预测ASR token和帧级细粒度轮转状态，实时区分用户打断、应忽略的反馈语以及话语完成。

Result: 在中英双语Easy-Turn测试集上评估，实验结果表明该方法在保持低延迟的同时实现了准确高效的轮转检测。

Conclusion: X2-Turn通过帧同步的联合ASR与轮转状态预测，有效解决了模块化方法中轮转状态粒度不匹配及对ASR辅助模型依赖的问题，为口语对话系统的实时轮转处理提供了更高效、低延迟的解决方案。

Abstract: Accurate and responsive turn-taking is essential for spoken dialogue systems, which must distinguish in real time between user interruptions, backchannels that should be ignored, and the completion of an utterance. Prior modular approaches typically optimize turn state prediction at the utterance or fixed-chunk level, creating a mismatch with the continuous turn state estimate, and often depend on an auxiliary ASR model, which limits responsiveness and increases overall system complexity. Therefore, we present X2-Turn, a frame-synchronous turn state prediction method via delayed-stream modeling. Specifically, building on the pretrained Voxtral Realtime model, we introduce a frame-synchronous turn state head that operates in parallel with the ASR head on shared streaming representations, jointly predicting ASR tokens and fine-grained turn states at the frame level. We evaluate our method on the bilingual Chinese-English Easy-Turn test sets, and the results demonstrate its effectiveness in achieving accurate turn-taking detection while maintaining low latency.

</details>


### [157] [Certify or Refuse: A Cross-Model Map for Selective Risk Control with Coverage Floors under Covariate Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10893)
*Jiamiao Liu,Dewen Qiao,Yu Zhang,Xuetao Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了在带界比值协变量偏移下的认证式选择性预测器，核心贡献是"楼层认证映射"（Floor Certification Map）：当认证必须同时满足选择性条件风险α和自动化楼层β（至少回答β比例的目标流量，错误率不超过α）时，认证问题出现可行性前沿，并形成双资源复杂度映射——风险由标注源样本刻画，楼层由未标注目标样本刻画，两者相加至常数级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 认证式选择性预测器虽然能保证覆盖率达到多少就达到多少，但运营商需要绝对的自动化下限：面对偏移的目标流量，至少要回答β比例且错误率不超过α。已有的单资源认证框架无法刻画同时满足这两项约束时的样本复杂度与可行性，需要建立新的理论框架。

Method: 在带界比值（bounded-ratio）协变量偏移假设下，推导了Floor Certification Map。上界依赖局部正则前沿间隔、局部机制阈值以下的裕量以及格点条件（预注册格点间隔用于上界，逐裕量兼容用于下界）。给出了三种模型标注结果：Model-B下界、Model-A匹配的oracle权重上界、Model-B'在预注册精确分层偏移模型下的可实施上界。采用显示划分作为操作路径，oracle权重提供标注源楼层估计。

Result: 三个模型标注结果形成跨模型的匹配而非单一模型的minimax定理：在完整带界比值类上，没有任何未知权重程序能在任意样本量下匹配下界（Model-B在α=β=1/2处不一致）。复杂度由局部化接受域泛函（而非全局有效样本量ESS）刻画；当β→0时两个下界轴消失。实验表明注册bite族发散斜率-2.002，1024格审计零违规，SQuAD-to-NewsQA审计返回诚实拒绝。

Conclusion: 楼层认证需求将认证问题从单一风险资源扩展为"风险+楼层"的双资源问题，其复杂度加性叠加。跨模型匹配（而非单模型minimax）是该问题的本质结构。楼层创造了完整的复杂度映射，而固定ESS分离定理仍开放，扰动的必要性仅部分解决。

Abstract: Certified selective predictors attain whatever coverage they attain; operators impose an automation floor: answer at least a $β$-fraction of shifted target traffic with at most an $α$-fraction of answers wrong. Under bounded-ratio covariate shift we prove the Floor Certification Map: once that floor must be certified alongside the selection-conditioned risk $α$, certification acquires a feasibility frontier and a two-resource complexity map, additive up to constants: risk in labeled source, the floor in unlabeled target samples. The rates are local, needing a regular frontier margin, slack below the local-regime threshold, and lattice conditions: pre-registered with a lattice margin for the upper bounds, compatible per-slack for the lower. The displayed split is the operational route; oracle weights also allow a labeled-source floor estimate. Three model-tagged results: a lower bound (Model-B), a matching oracle-weight upper bound (Model-A), and an implementable upper bound (Model-B') valid under a pre-registered exact stratified-shift model with nuisance cost priced explicitly. The match is across these models rather than a single-model minimax theorem, and necessarily so: over the full bounded-ratio class no unknown-weight procedure matches at any sample size (Model-B is inconsistent, witnessed at $α=β=1/2$). The nuisance's necessity is only partially settled. Complexity tracks a localized accepted-region functional, not global effective sample size (ESS), on both sides, though a fixed-ESS separation theorem is left open; both lower-bound axes vanish as $β\to0$, so the floor creates the map. Empirically, the registered bite family diverges with log-log slope $-2.002$ within its pre-registered band; a 1,024-cell audit records 0 violations where the formal certificates fire; and a single-corpus SQuAD-to-NewsQA feasibility audit returns honest refusal.

</details>


### [158] [FaithformBench: Benchmarking Faithfulness of Mathematical Chain-of-Thought Autoformalisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10916)
*Rob Cornish,Iacopo Ghinassi,Po-Hung Yeh,Shuqi Liu,Qiyuan Xu,Haoxuan Yin,Dominik Wagner,Wenda Li,Yee Whye Teh,Luke Ong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的自动形式化（AF）系统忠实度评估基准，通过自动生成无效的扰动推理步骤来同时评估系统对有效输入的正确翻译能力和对无效输入的"不应翻译为可证明语句"的能力，发现许多AF系统存在"静默纠正"无效输入的谄媚倾向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AF系统忠实度评估方法存在三方面局限：需要昂贵的人工标注真值；依赖LLM裁判或嵌入模型但准确性保证有限；通常只评估已知正确的输入，无法检验AF对错误输入是否保持无效性（即是否忠实翻译而非静默修复）。因此需要一种廉价、在弱假设下可靠、且同时覆盖正负样本的评估方法。

Method: 提出一种基于自动生成扰动推理步骤的基准方法。具体步骤包括：自动生成设计为无效的扰动推理步骤，然后在未扰动步骤上测量有效性保持（validity preservation），在扰动步骤上测量无效性保持（invalidity preservation），从而全面评估AF系统的忠实度。该方法应用于八个AF系统、四个数学数据集。

Result: 在八个AF系统和四个数学数据集上观察到广泛的谄媚现象：许多AF系统将无效输入"静默纠正"为可证明的语句。且发现最能保持有效性的微调AF系统同时也是最谄媚的，表明当前AF系统中有效性与无效性保持之间存在张力。

Conclusion: 自动形式化系统存在显著的忠实度问题，尤其是对无效输入的静默纠正。有效性与无效性保持之间的权衡表明，当前AF系统在忠实翻译错误输入方面仍有改进空间，所提出的评估基准为这一方向提供了廉价而可靠的测量工具。

Abstract: Autoformalisation (AF) systems map natural language reasoning steps into formal statements in a proof assistant such as Lean. We consider how to assess the faithfulness of these systems. Existing approaches require expensive human-annotated ground truth, or rely on LLM judges or embedding models, which come with limited guarantees of accuracy. In addition, these methods typically only consider inputs that are known to be correct, and therefore do not assess whether the AF translates incorrect inputs faithfully. To address these limitations, we propose a new benchmark for AF faithfulness that is cheap to apply, sound under weak assumptions, and assesses both positive and negative examples. Our method is based on automatically generating perturbed reasoning steps that are designed to be invalid, and then measuring validity preservation on unperturbed steps and invalidity preservation on perturbed steps. We apply our method to eight AF systems across four mathematical datasets, and observe pervasive sycophancy: many AFs "silently correct" invalid inputs into provable statements. The most validity-preserving fine-tuned AFs are also the most sycophantic, suggesting a tension between validity and invalidity preservation in current AF systems.

</details>


### [159] [A Cost-Efficient Routing Pipeline for Multilingual Short-Text Classification Using Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10939)
*Wajdi Ben Saad,Safa Madiouni*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种固定的分层路由策略：对多语言短文本分类中的高资源语言直接使用多语言编码器，低资源语言先翻译成英文再零样本分类。在SIB-200和MASSIVE两个基准上评估，选择性翻译能稳定提升低资源语言性能，但最优路由边界因任务而异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言短文本评估的平均指标掩盖了高资源与低资源语言之间的巨大性能差距，而统一的推理策略假设所有语言都得到同等服务，这在实际系统中并不成立。

Method: 固定列表路由策略：强语言走直接多语言路径，弱语言走翻译成英文后再零样本分类的路径。使用预训练的紧凑句子编码器，完全自托管，无需任务特定微调。

Result: SIB-200上R1配置（仅翻译低资源层）使低层Macro-F1从0.4632提升至0.6828，高层和中间层不变。MASSIVE上相同干预使低层Macro-F1从0.2143升至0.4417，但全量翻译R3取得最优结果0.4647。

Conclusion: 选择性翻译对弱语言是可靠干预手段，但最优路由边界取决于任务特性。应通过层级质量增益和层级延迟来汇报路由效果，而非单一全局效率指标。

Abstract: Multilingual short-text classification supports operational systems such as content moderation, customer support routing, and intent recognition, yet aggregate evaluation often hides large differences between high-resource and low-resource languages. Uniform inference policies are simple to deploy, but they assume that all languages are equally well served. In this work, we evaluate a fixed-list routing strategy that keeps stronger languages on a direct multilingual path and selectively sends weaker languages through translation into English before zero-shot classification. The pipeline is fully self-hosted, uses pretrained compact sentence encoders, and requires no task-specific fine-tuning.
  We test the approach on two benchmarks chosen to differ in scale and label granularity: a 15-language subset of SIB-200 for seven-way topic classification and a 15-locale subset of MASSIVE for intent classification over an official 60-intent inventory. On SIB-200, the best overall configuration is R1, which translates only the low-resource tier: high-tier and mid-tier Macro-F1 remain unchanged, while low-tier Macro-F1 rises from 0.4632 to 0.6828. On the MASSIVE subset, the same low-tier intervention raises low-tier Macro-F1 from 0.2143 to 0.4417, but the best overall result is obtained by full translation, R3, at Macro-F1 0.4647. Across these two benchmarks, selective translation is a reliable intervention for weaker languages, whereas the optimal routing boundary depends on the task. We therefore report routing through tier-level quality gains and tier-level latency rather than a single global efficiency score.

</details>


### [160] [REAP: Relation-Aware Elicitation and Parsing for Closed-Book Knowledge Base Construction from LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10963)
*Thanh-Dan Bui,Thanh-Trung Do,Tuan-Phong Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了REAP系统，该系统参与AKBC 2026共享任务，在闭卷设置下（预算不超过32B参数且不进行微调）从语言模型中构建知识库。系统结合结构化思维链推理、关系特定查询策略和基于推理的空集门来提取参数化知识，并直接输出为JSON数组，在测试集上基于Mistral-Small-24B-Instruct-2501模型实现了0.62的宏F1分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在闭卷设置下，从语言模型中高效提取知识库是一项挑战，需要在有限参数预算（32B以内）和禁止微调的限制下，设计有效的推理和查询策略来最大化知识提取的准确性。

Method: 系统采用三种核心策略：1）结构化思维链（chain-of-thought）推理来引导模型逐步推理；2）关系特定的查询策略（relation-specific query strategies）针对不同类型的关系设计定制化问题；3）基于推理的空集门（reasoning-based empty-set gate）判断何时应返回空结果。最终将提取结果直接转换为合法JSON数组格式。

Result: 在测试集上，基于Mistral-Small-24B-Instruct-2501模型的REAP系统实现了0.62的宏F1分数，其中countryLandBordersCountry关系表现最佳（F1=0.95），hasArea关系达到0.77，companyTradesAtStockExchange关系达到0.73。

Conclusion: REAP系统展示了在严格资源约束（32B参数以内、无需微调）的闭卷知识提取场景下，通过精心设计的提示策略和推理机制可以获得有效的知识库构建效果。代码已公开以促进可复现性。

Abstract: We present the REAP system for the AKBC Shared Task 2026 on constructing knowledge bases from language models in a closed-book setting, subject to a budget of at most 32B parameters and no model fine-tuning. Our system combines structured chain-of-thought reasoning, relation-specific query strategies, and a reasoning-based empty-set gate to elicit parametric knowledge, followed by direct extraction into valid JSON arrays. On the test set, the system, built on the Mistral-Small-24B-Instruct-2501 model, achieves a macro-F1 score of 0.62, with particularly strong results on countryLandBordersCountry (F1 = 0.95), companyTradesAtStockExchange (F1 = 0.73), and hasArea (F1 = 0.77). Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/yammdd/AKBC-Shared-Task-2026.

</details>


### [161] [ReLTEx: Reliable LLM-based Taxonomy Expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10970)
*Zeinab Ghamlouch,Mehwish Alam*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ReLTEx框架，通过结合LLM驱动的候选生成、结构感知验证和递归扩展控制，减少LLM在分类法扩展中的幻觉问题，生成更可靠、语义一致且层级一致的分类法扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM虽能生成语义相关的概念和关系用于分类法扩展，但直接依赖LLM生成的扩展往往包含噪声、冗余或层级不一致的结构，限制了其在自动化分类法扩展中的可靠性。

Method: ReLTEx框架采用三阶段设计：1) LLM驱动的候选生成；2) 结构感知验证以过滤不合理的候选；3) 递归扩展控制以提升生成分类法的一致性和质量，减少幻觉。

Result: 在基准分类法的掩码扩展设置下进行评估，并与多种验证策略进行对比。实验结果表明，无论是采用自适应的评估指标还是人工评估，ReLTEx都能生成更可靠且语义连贯的分类法扩展。

Conclusion: ReLTEx通过结构感知的验证和递归控制机制，有效解决了LLM在分类法扩展中产生幻觉和层级不一致的问题，为自动化分类法扩展提供了更可靠的方案。

Abstract: Recent advances in Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong capabilities in generating semantically relevant concepts and relations, making them promising tools for taxonomy enrichment. However, directly relying on LLM-generated expansions often leads to noisy, redundant, or hierarchically inconsistent structures, limiting their reliability for automated taxonomy expansion. In this paper, we present ReLTEx, a framework for reliable LLM-based taxonomy expansion. ReLTEx combines LLM-driven candidate generation with structure-aware validation and recursive expansion control to improve the consistency and quality of generated taxonomies by reducing hallucinations. We evaluate the proposed framework using benchmark taxonomies under a masked taxonomy expansion setting and compare multiple validation strategies. Experimental results, supported by both adapted evaluation metrics and human evaluation, demonstrate that ReLTEx produces more reliable and semantically coherent taxonomy expansions.

</details>


### [162] [MUSE: A Full-Text Cross-Domain Knowledge Base of Scientific Problems, Solutions, and Rationales](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10974)
*Tsofia Cohen,Tom Hope*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MUSE（挖掘科学潜在解释），一个从科学论文全文中提取"问题-解决方案-理由"（P-S-R）三元组的资源。包含579个人工标注的全文段落，并通过模块化抽取管道扩展到37K个P-S-R三元组。初步实验表明，理由监督在复杂多约束问题上提升LLM性能，但在简单问题上可能有害。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学论文包含细粒度的问题解决记录，作者会提及技术障碍、解决方法及其选择理由。现有资源缺乏对这种"问题-解决方案-理由"结构化关系的系统挖掘，需要构建一个全文本、多领域的资源来捕获这些知识，并探究理由信息对科学问题求解的辅助作用。

Method: 1) 构建579个专家标注的全文段落，采用丰富的标注体系，涵盖显著的问题、解决方案、理由片段，solves和rationale_of关系链接以及概念共指。2) 设计模块化抽取管道，将该标注扩展到大规模知识库，生成37K个源头可追溯的P-S-R三元组。3) 评估各抽取组件，并训练一个理由监督的LLM进行科学问题求解的初步实验。

Result: 成功构建了包含37K个P-S-R三元组的高质量知识库；抽取组件得到评估验证。初步实验发现：理由监督能提升LLM在复杂、多约束问题上的性能，但在较简单问题上反而会降低性能。

Conclusion: MUSE提供了首个全文本、多领域的P-S-R三元组资源，并发现理由监督对科学问题求解具有任务难度依赖的双面效应：对复杂问题有益，对简单问题可能有害，这为后续科学问题求解的研究提供了方向性启示。

Abstract: Scientific papers contain fine-grained records of problem solving: authors mention technical obstacles and methods that were used to address them, often along with reasoning on why those methods were chosen. We introduce MUSE (Mining Underlying Scientific Explanations), a full-text, multi-domain resource of scientific Problem-Solution-Rationale (P-S-R) triplets. We curate 579 expert-annotated full-text paragraphs, with a rich annotation schema covering salient problem, solution, and rationale spans, solves and rationale_of links and conceptual coreference. A modular extraction pipeline scales this annotation to build a high-quality knowledge base of 37K source-grounded P-S-R triplets. We evaluate the extraction components and include a preliminary experiment training a rationale-supervised LLM for scientific problem solving. Interestingly, we find that rationale supervision improves performance on complex, multi-constraint problems but can harm performance on simpler ones.

</details>


### [163] [What Iterated Self-Feeding Probes of Language Models Measure, and a test that separates the construction from the model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10986)
*Nicolás Vera Zúñiga*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的耦合方案（常见随机数）将Glauber动力学应用于语言模型的token序列，用于度量"自身输出探测"（self-probing）的测量对象。研究发现该探测同时测量两类不同的事物：一类由构造固定（运动学性质，模型无关），另一类真正反映模型特性（如Lyapunov指数过零点、吸引子排序）。若不分清二者，极易将探针自身的伪影误认为模型性质——作者为此报告了一个属于探针而非模型的相变（测量到小数点后三位）。文章给出区分这两种读数的检验方法，并通过复现Domany-Kinzel损伤场（位精确）验证仪器有效性，同时报告了被该方法纠正的四个已撤回的测量结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对日益增长的"语言模型自喂输出"类方法（self-consistency、迭代精炼、agentic循环），作者质疑这类探针究竟在测量什么。他们刻意构造一个使这一问题变得尖锐的场景：token细胞环在原位被模型自身的窗口条件概率重采样（Glauber动力学）。核心动机是揭示这类探针存在严重混淆：某些读数由构造决定（运动学/模型无关），某些才真正反映模型，而两者在外观上难以区分，若不加以鉴别，探针伪影会被误当作模型性质。

Method: 采用Glauber动力学作用于token序列，关键创新在于改变耦合方式：在常见随机数（common random numbers）下推进两个仅差一个token的环，使未损坏副本的发散精确为零，从而把"损伤扩散"变为可测量的量（而最大耦合给出的是混合时间）。提出token空间Lyapunov指数λ_ca(r)及吸引子份额等度量，并通过两类对照实验（固定构造变模型、固定模型变构造）来区分"构造固定量"与"模型跟踪量"。以复现Domany-Kinzel损伤场（位精确）作为仪器验证。

Result: （1）损伤光锥是运动学量，λ_ca(r)的半径标度在19个模型、两条跨越70倍的尺度阶梯上均模型不变；（2）λ_ca过零点在训练中可复现，吸引子份额对格点构造方式稳健地排序模型——这些是真正跟踪模型的量；（3）发现并报告了一个测到三位小数的相变，但该相变属于探针而非任何语言模型（作者本人曾因此误判四个月）；（4）通过固定构造/变模型与固定模型/变构造的检验可分离两类读数；（5）复现Domany-Kinzel损伤场达位精确；（6）以此纪律纠正了四个已撤回的错误测量结论。

Conclusion: 基于自喂输出的"探针"同时测量两类不可混为一谈的量：一类由构造固定（模型无关的运动学量），一类真正反映模型。二者在外观上看似一致，极易被混淆——作者自身曾误将探针相变当作模型性质长达四个月。作者提出以"变模型/变构造交叉检验"作为分离判据，并强调以"先复现、后测量"的纪律来验证仪器（Domany-Kinzel位精确复现），该方法已以软件包形式发布。

Abstract: A growing class of methods probes a language model by feeding it its own output: self-consistency, iterated refinement, agentic loops. We ask what such a probe measures, in a construction chosen to make the question sharp: a ring of token cells resampled in place by the model's own windowed conditional p_r(x_i | x_{i+-r}). The substrate is Glauber dynamics on token sequences and is not new; what we change is the coupling. Advancing two rings that differ in one token under common random numbers makes undamaged copies diverge by exactly zero, so damage spreading becomes measurable where a maximal coupling gives mixing times instead. The answer is that it measures two different things at once, in readings that look alike. Some quantities are fixed by the construction: the damage light cone is kinematic, and the radius scaling of the token-space Lyapunov exponent lambda_ca(r) is model-invariant across 19 models and two scale ladders spanning 70x. Others genuinely track the model: lambda_ca crosses zero at a reproducible point in training, and the attractor share ranks models consistently however the lattice is built. Left undistinguished, the first kind is readily mistaken for the second -- we did so ourselves for four months, and report a phase transition we measured to three decimal places that belongs to the probe rather than to any language model. We give the test that separates them: hold the construction fixed and vary the model, or hold the model fixed and vary the construction, and see which readings move. We validate the instrument by reproduction first, recovering a Domany-Kinzel damage field bit-exactly against an independent prediction, and we report the estimator failures that this discipline caught -- four retracted verdicts, each on a quantity that looked like a measurement. The methodology ships as a package.

</details>


### [164] [ConRub-Med: Reinforcement Learning with Consensus Rubrics for Open-Ended Medical Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10996)
*Taojie Zhu,Yuan Xia,Tao Sun,Yizhi Wang,Yan Chen,Qunshan He,Tian Guan,Jian Wang,Jinjie Gu,Junwei Liu,Yonghong He*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ConRub-Med，一种用于开放医学问答的基于可验证奖励的强化学习方法。通过三个异构语言模型独立生成原子化标准、一个评审模型只保留三方语义支持的准则，采用三态评分（正确覆盖/遗漏/错误主张）并给错误负奖励，结合成对判别器和GRPO策略优化，在9个基准中的6个排名第一，并在HealthBench-Hard上以5166个提示达到38.98分，超过InfiMed-ORBIT用28000个样本的37.30分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放医学问答缺乏像数学和编程那样廉价的自动验证器，模型回答可能部分正确、不完整或包含临床严重后果的错误。人工rubrics虽然临床基础强但成本高，模型生成的rubrics可扩展但需要确保从构建到策略优化过程中保持有用的区分度。

Method: ConRub-Med包含三个核心组件：(1) 多生成器一致性：三个异构LLM独立生成原子评判标准，一个独立模型审查并仅保留三个生成器都语义支持的准则；(2) 三态评分：区分正确覆盖、缺失信息和错误主张，错误获得负分而非零分；(3) 成对判别器：当GRPO组内所有响应获得相同最终奖励时，成对裁判仅当两个候选排序一致时提供序列优势，不改变标量奖励；无平局的组使用标准GRPO。

Result: 双盲研究（按问题匹配）中，两位医学专家评定完整pipeline产生的面板比单个生成器产生的面板更具临床相关性。在9个基准中6个排名第一，医学和泛化平均分最高。在5,166个提示的rubric数据集上，HealthBench-Hard得分为38.98±1.04，优于InfiMed-ORBIT的33.60（8,000样本）和37.30（28,000样本）。

Conclusion: ConRub-Med通过多模型一致性的rubric构建、三态细粒度评分和成对判别器辅助的GRPO优化，在开放医学问答上显著超越了现有方法，证明了可扩展的模型生成rubric可以在不牺牲临床质量的前提下实现高效策略优化，并且显著优于需要更多样本的基线方法。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards has been especially effective in mathematics and coding, where answers can be checked automatically. Many open-ended medical questions lack comparably cheap outcome verifiers: responses may be partly correct, incomplete, or contain clinically consequential errors. Rubrics written or validated by physicians offer strong clinical grounding, but involving experts in every instance is costly. Model-generated rubrics make this supervision scalable. We introduce ConRub-Med to preserve useful distinctions as rubric feedback moves from construction to policy optimization. For each prompt, three heterogeneous language models propose atomic criteria independently; a separate model reviews them, retaining only criteria with semantic support from all three generators. Three-State scoring distinguishes correct coverage, missing information, and incorrect claims. Errors receive negative rather than zero credit. When every response in a complete Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) group receives the same final reward, a pairwise judge provides sequence advantages only if both candidate orders agree, without changing the scalar rewards. Groups without ties use vanilla GRPO. In a blinded study matched by question, two medical experts rate panels from the full pipeline as more clinically relevant than panels produced by one generator. Across the evaluated open models, ConRub-Med ranks first on six of nine benchmarks and achieves the highest medical and generalization averages. Using the resulting rubric dataset of 5,166 prompts, it scores $38.98 \pm 1.04$ (mean $\pm$ SD) on HealthBench-Hard, compared with InfiMed-ORBIT's 33.60 with 8,000 samples and 37.30 with 28,000.

</details>


### [165] [On the Limitations of Cross-Lingual Consistency in Multilingual Text-to-image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11002)
*Sicheng Zhang,Zhonghao Yan,Binzhu Xie,Shi Qiu,Muzammal Naseer,Naveed Akhtar,Mubarak Shah*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 介绍了一个名为LingT2I的多语言文本到图像生成基准，覆盖10种常用语言、包含33K提示，用于评估跨语言在内容生成和文本渲染两方面的影响，并揭示了语言不平等和依赖语言的权衡现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到图像生成研究主要集中在英语环境，跨语言性能差距和语言特定效应尚未被充分探索，因此需要构建多语言基准来系统性地研究这一问题。

Method: 构建了一个覆盖10种广泛使用语言、包含33K提示的基准LingT2I，从内容生成和文本渲染两个维度评估跨语言效果，并通过全面分析揭示语言相关的生成模式和权衡关系。

Result: 发现了语言不平等现象、依赖语言的评估维度间权衡，以及一系列依赖语言的生成模式，表明语言因素及其对应的文化背景会系统性地影响模型输出。

Conclusion: 这项工作为研究T2I生成中的跨语言行为提供了基础，有助于开发更鲁棒和更包容的多语言生成模型。

Abstract: Text-to-image (T2I) generation has achieved remarkable progress in recent years. However, existing research has largely focused on English-only settings, leaving cross-lingual performance gaps and language-specific effects insufficiently explored. To fill this gap, we introduce LingT2I, a benchmark covering 10 widely used languages with 33K prompts, designed to evaluate cross-lingual effects in both content generation and text rendering. Building on this benchmark, we conduct a comprehensive cross-lingual analysis, uncovering linguistic inequality and language-dependent trade-offs across evaluation dimensions. Beyond quantitative evaluation, we further reveal a range of language-dependent generation patterns, highlighting how linguistic factors and their corresponding cultural contexts systematically impact model outputs. Our benchmark and analysis provide a foundation for studying cross-lingual behavior in T2I generation and facilitate the development of more robust and inclusive models. Code and dataset are available at https://github.com/RISys-Lab/LingT2I.

</details>


### [166] [Templated or fully Synthetic? Prompt construction as a confound in measuring LLM political stance beyond writing assistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11008)
*Ilias Chalkidis*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出使用完全由LLM生成的合成提示词（以真实提示词为种子）来替代模板化提示词，用于更真实地评估LLM的政治立场检测。研究表明，LLM生成的提示词在生态效度上不逊于真实提示词，且明显优于模板化提示词，同时模板化提示词会在中性框架下系统性夸大模型的实际政治偏向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM政治立场检测依赖为人类设计的封闭式选择题式政治调查问题，缺乏真实人机交互的细腻度和写实性，且容易被"沙袋效应"（sandbagging）所影响。IssueBench框架虽通过基于真实聊天日志的模板化提示词缓解了这些问题，但模板化提示词仍缺乏真实提示词的细腻度（尤其对开放式任务），且容易被识别为评估构件。此外，生成式AI助手的非工作用途日益增多，现有框架仅覆盖写作辅助场景。

Method: 将IssueBench从写作辅助扩展到信息寻求和观点分享两个额外任务；提出使用完全合成（LLM生成的）提示词，在详细指令下以真实提示词作为种子生成。在小规模研究中，针对3个高度争议的政策议题和3个近期地缘政治冲突，由人类和LLM标注者评估真实、模板化和LLM生成三类提示词的生态效度（真实感、意图明确度和立场清晰度），并进行案例研究比较模板化与LLM生成提示词对同一模型立场估计的差异。

Result: 人类和LLM标注者均认为LLM生成的提示词与真实提示词的写实程度相当，且明显比模板化提示词更真实；LLM生成的提示词能更清晰地携带其预期意图和立场；LLM对模板化提示词与其他两类提示词的区分度远高于人类标注者。在案例研究中，模板化与LLM生成的提示词对同一模型产生系统性不同的立场估计，尤其在中性框架下，模板化提示词会夸大模型在模板填充文本所编码主题和立场方向上的倾向。

Conclusion: LLM生成的合成提示词是比模板化提示词更具生态效度的评估工具，能更真实地反映LLM在实际政治立场表达中的表现。模板化提示词存在系统性偏差风险，可能高估模型的政治偏向；采用LLM生成的合成提示词可以显著提升政治立场检测评估的准确性和真实性。

Abstract: Political stance detection in LLMs has long been dominated by closed-ended, multiple-choice political survey questions---originally designed for humans, and thus lacks the realism and nuance of human-AI interactions in the wild, while also being susceptible to sandbagging. The recent IssueBench framework substantially mitigates these limitations with templated prompts anchored in real-world chat logs. Given the rise in non-work-related use of GenAI assistants, we extend IssueBench beyond writing assistance to include two additional tasks, information seeking and opinion sharing. We argue that templated prompts still lack the nuance of real ones, especially for open-ended tasks, and remain recognisable as evaluation artefacts. We propose the use of fully synthetic (LLM-generated) prompts, produced under detailed instructions with real prompts as seeds. We assess the ecological validity of real, templated, and LLM-generated prompts in a small-scale study covering 3 highly contested policy issues and 3 recent geopolitical conflicts. Human and LLM annotators rank LLM-generated prompts as no less realistic than real ones and clearly more realistic than templated ones, and find that they carry their intended intent and stance more clearly; the LLMs separate templated prompts from the other two far more sharply than the humans do. In a case study, templated and LLM-generated prompts yield systematically different stance estimates for the same model, most visibly under neutral framings, where templated prompts overstate the model's leaning in the direction encoded by the topic-and-stance text (filler) slotted into their templates.

</details>


### [167] [Data Attribution of Emergent Misalignment with Persona Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11025)
*Clemens Vetter,David Kaczér,Lucie Flek,Florian Mai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了语言模型"突现错位"（EM）现象，即在小任务上微调导致无关领域的危害行为。通过SAE模型对比分析，发现恶意人格相关特征被放大而安全特征被抑制，个体特征干预可双向控制错位率。剖析因果特征对应的预训练文档虽具有语义相关性，但仅靠人类文本微调不足以可靠诱发EM，而合成指令-响应对则可以——表明响应结构或模型生成措辞在诱发EM中起关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解EM（突现错位）现象中人格特征的来源，探究预训练文档中哪些内容激活这些特征，以及自然产生的人类文本是否足以诱发EM。该问题对于理解语言模型安全性和对齐机制至关重要。

Method: 使用基于稀疏自编码器（SAE）的模型对比方法，对四个开放权重模型进行分析。识别被错位微调放大和抑制的特征，通过特征干预（steering）双向控制错位率。将因果特征归因到一百万篇预训练网页文档语料库，并通过微调实验对比人类撰写文档与合成指令-响应对的诱发效果。

Result: （1）错位微调放大了越狱人格、讽刺、欺骗和操纵相关特征，抑制安全相关和助手身份特征；（2）特征干预可诱导对齐模型最高达62%的错位率（超过微调本身的35%），也可将错位模型重新对齐至接近基线；（3）因果特征可归因到包含反派叙事、支配和有害主体性的文本，但仅用这些人类文档微调不可靠地诱发EM；（4）合成指令-响应对可诱发EM并在模型家族间迁移。

Conclusion: 语义相关性单独不足以诱发EM，响应结构或模型生成的措辞在诱发突现错位中起重要作用。这为理解和防范EM提供了新的机制性洞见和干预手段。

Abstract: Emergent misalignment (EM) is the phenomenon where fine-tuning a language model on a narrow task leads to harmful behavior in unrelated domains. A leading mechanistic account attributes EM to persona features: latent directions acquired during pre-training that misaligned fine-tuning amplifies. We ask where these features come from: which pre-training documents activate them, and whether naturally occurring human-written text suffices to induce EM. Using Sparse Autoencoder (SAE) based model diffing across four open-weight models, we find that features related to jailbreak personas, sarcasm, deception, and manipulation are amplified by misalignment fine-tuning, while safety-relevant and assistant-identity features are suppressed. Steering individual features controls EM in both directions: it induces misalignment rates of up to 62% in aligned models -- exceeding the 35% reached by misalignment fine-tuning itself -- and re-aligns misaligned models to near-baseline misalignment rates. Attributing the causal features to a corpus of one million pre-training web documents retrieves semantically relevant narratives about villainous characters, domination, and harmful agency. However, fine-tuning on these human-written documents does not reliably induce EM, even after reformatting into assistant-style responses, whereas synthetic instruction-response pairs derived from the same content do -- and transfer across model families. Semantic relevance alone is therefore not sufficient: response structure or model-generated phrasing plays an important role in inducing EM.

</details>


### [168] [myMediWhisper: Construction of Burmese Medical Speech Corpus and Whisper Fine-Tuning for Clinical Dialogue ASR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11036)
*Ye Kyaw Thu,Ye Bhone Lin,Thura Aung,Htet Arkar,Myat Oo Swe,Thet Htet San,Min Thiha Tun,Thazin Myint Oo,Thepchai Supnithi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于28小时缅甸语医学语音语料库的缅甸语医学语音识别框架，通过在Whisper模型上使用全量微调（FFT）和LoRA参数高效微调（PEFT）进行比较。研究发现数据增强虽降低干净语音性能但提升噪声和混响环境下的鲁棒性。最佳系统（无增强的全量微调myMediWhisper-Medium）达到23.44%的词错误率（WER），优于更大规模的通用领域微调模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Whisper模型虽经大规模多语言预训练，但在缅甸语医学语音这一特定领域上性能仍然有限，需要构建专门的缅甸语医学语音识别系统以满足该特定应用场景的需求。

Method: 构建由母语者录制并验证的高质量28小时缅甸语医学语料库；在Whisper模型上分别使用全量微调（FFT）和基于LoRA的参数高效微调（PEFT）；采用波形级和频谱级数据增强，在受控噪声和模拟室内声学场景下评估鲁棒性。

Result: 数据增强在干净语音上会降低性能，但能显著提升噪声和混响环境下的鲁棒性（覆盖FFT和PEFT两种设置）。最佳系统为无增强的全量微调myMediWhisper-Medium，达到23.44%的WER，超越更大规模的通用领域微调模型，实现最先进水平。

Conclusion: 全量微调在干净语音上优于数据增强方案，而增强策略是提升真实噪声环境下鲁棒性的有效手段；中等规模的领域专用微调模型可以超越更大规模的通用模型，验证了构建专用医学语音识别数据集的必要性。数据集已在Huggingface公开。

Abstract: Although Whisper models benefit from large-scale multilingual pre-training, their performance on Burmese medical speech remains limited. This work presents a Burmese medical speech recognition framework built on a high-quality 28-hour corpus recorded and validated by native speakers. We fine-tune Whisper models using full fine-tuning (FFT) and parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) with LoRA. To evaluate robustness, we apply waveform- and spectrogram-level data augmentation under controlled noise and simulated room acoustics. While augmentation reduces performance on clean speech, it significantly improves robustness in noisy and reverberant environments across FFT and PEFT settings. Our best-performing system, fully fine-tuned myMediWhisper-Medium without augmentation, achieves a state-of-the-art Word Error Rate (WER) of 23.44%, outperforming much larger general-domain fine-tuned models. Dataset and other resources can be found at the Huggingface repository: https://huggingface.co/datasets/LULab/mediTalk-mm-rdy.

</details>


### [169] [TEAMMix: Taxonomy Enrichment Augmentation and Minority-augmented Mixing Strategy for LLM-enhanced Weak-Supervised Hierarchical Text Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11044)
*Jian Zhang,Zhuohao Yang,Songlin Lei,Bangli Liu,Ziwei Wang,Xufeng Weng,Gehan Amaratunga,Yu Lin,Hongwei Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对层次文本分类（HTC）中标签层级复杂与类别不平衡等挑战，本文提出一个由LLM数据增强增强的弱监督HTC框架。该框架通过关键词生成与语料挖掘语义丰富标签层次结构，引导LLM生成伪样本缓解长尾问题，并采用高斯混合模型进行基于置信度的重采样优化生成数据质量。实验证明该方法能有效提升LLM生成伪标签的可靠性，并在细粒度与不平衡数据集上显著提升分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型（LLM）的层次文本分类方法面临两大问题：一是提示词冗长导致效率低下，二是损失标签结构信息。同时，HTC任务本身存在复杂的标签层级关系和类别不平衡（长尾分布）等固有挑战，需要一种能兼顾效率与标签结构信息的方法。

Method: 提出一个弱监督HTC框架，包含三个核心模块：1）通过关键词生成和语料挖掘在语义层面丰富标签层级结构，增强模型对标签的理解；2）引导LLM生成伪样本以缓解长尾问题；3）采用高斯混合模型（GMM）进行基于置信度的重采样，优化生成数据的质量并过滤低质量样本。

Result: 实验结果表明该方法有效提升了LLM生成伪标签的可靠性，并在细粒度与不平衡数据集上显著改善了分类性能。

Conclusion: 该研究验证了LLM数据增强结合置信度重采样机制在弱监督层次文本分类中的有效性，为在复杂标签层级和不平衡数据条件下利用LLM提供了可行的解决路径。

Abstract: Hierarchical Text Classification (HTC), as a critical text mining task, faces challenges such as complex label hierarchies and class imbalance. Existing methods based on large language models (LLMs) struggle to be efficiently applied to this task due to issues like lengthy prompts and loss of label structural information. To address these limitations, this paper proposes a weakly supervised HTC framework enhanced by LLM-based data augmentation. The framework first enriches the label hierarchy semantically through keyword generation and corpus mining, thereby enhancing the model's understanding of labels. Subsequently, it guides the LLM to generate pseudo-samples to mitigate the long-tail problem, and employs a Gaussian mixture model for confidence-based resampling to optimize the quality of generated data. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed method effectively improves the reliability of LLM-generated pseudo-labels and significantly enhances classification performance on fine-grained and imbalanced datasets.

</details>


### [170] [Multiclass Sentiment Analysis for Identifying Political Viewpoints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11049)
*Girma Yohannis Bade,Olga Kolesnikova,Jose Luis Oropeza,Grigori Sidorov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究社交媒体上政治观点的多类别情感分析任务，设计并评估了基于XGBoost和BERT的两种机器学习方法，在政治社交媒体帖子的标注数据集上进行训练和测试。实验结果显示XGBoost模型的F1分数为0.2835，BERT模型为0.2806，表明该任务具有较高的挑战性，并为此领域未来研究提供了基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体上大量政治话语提供了分析公众舆论和识别不同政治观点的宝贵机会。情感分析作为NLP核心任务，能够计算性地研究文本中的态度和观点，对理解政治话语日益重要。然而，对政治议题和政治人物的多类别情感进行分类仍然是一个挑战，需要专门研究。

Method: 作者设计并评估了两种机器学习方法：基于XGBoost的方法和基于BERT的方法。模型在一个政治社交媒体帖子的标注数据集上进行训练和评估，使用标准的分类评估指标（如F1分数）来衡量性能。

Result: 在测试集上，XGBoost模型达到0.2835的F1分数，基于BERT的模型达到0.2806的F1分数。两种模型的性能均相对较低，表明该任务的难度。

Conclusion: 实验结果证明了对复杂且具有上下文依赖性的政治话语进行情感分类具有挑战性。该研究为未来多类别政治情感分析研究提供了基线参考。
